# Boutique and Train-Ticket test splits (second look, exploratory)

The cohorts were scored previously. This report describes a second look. All intervals are unadjusted 95% start-node cluster bootstrap intervals.

| online_boutique | 12/14 | ppr | 14/14 | -0.1429 [-0.2857, +0.0000] | yes |
| train_ticket | 12/14 | community_graphrag | 7/14 | +0.3571 [+0.1429, +0.6429] | no |
